## 🤔 O que é o Seedance 2.5?

**Seedance 2.5** é o modelo de geração de vídeo de próxima geração da **ByteDance**, disponível via **Volcano Ark (火山方舟)**. Os prompts neste repositório são um **conjunto oficial de preview/amostra** publicado pela ByteDance / Volcano Ark para demonstrar os recursos do Seedance 2.5.

> ⚠️ **O Seedance 2.5 ainda está em lançamento gradual e ainda não está disponível para todos.** Estes são prompts oficiais de preview, **não contribuições da comunidade**. Prompts enviados pela comunidade serão adicionados aqui após a disponibilidade geral.

**Destaques apresentados neste preview:**

* 🎬 **Geração nativa de formato longo** — durações estendidas em plano único
* 🖼️ **Entrada com múltiplas referências** — várias imagens de referência por geração
* 📐 **Resolução até 4K**
* 🔊 **Dublagem e trilha automáticas** — narração e música de fundo automáticas
* 🎨 **Animação de texto criativa, UGC e vitrines de produtos**

### Craft competition testing segment in a traditional Japanese archery hall…

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Craft competition testing segment in a traditional Japanese archery hall, multi-camera documentary production, bright daylight through screens plus fluorescent tubes, sharp HD broadcast look, natural handheld shake on action cams, clean documentary texture, no cinematic film look.

An indoor wooden-floored archery hall. One entire wall is a lattice screen letting in flat white light; white plaster walls with exposed dark timber posts.

THE BOW: an asymmetric longbow far taller than a person, the grip set about one third up from the bottom so the upper limb is much longer. Laminated bamboo finished in dark red lacquer, bound with rattan at intervals. A pale twisted string.

0-2s: [Medium Two-Shot] A master archer in his 40s in a black training top, dark hakama and white split-toe socks faces the young female bowyer in her early 20s. He wears a leather chest guard and a deerskin glove on his right hand. She is in a navy work jacket and apron, hair tied back, unsmiling. He stands her bow upright and turns it once in the window light so the lacquer flashes, then meets her eye: "Are you ready?" She answers firmly "Yes." Quick cut to her determined face, then to three ceramic beckoning-cat figurines on stands across the hall, white bodies painted with red ears and collars and gold bells.

2-4s: [Low-Angle Tracking + Rapid Cuts] The master raises the bow high above his head, then spreads his arms and lowers it. His left arm extends straight forward while his gloved right hand draws the string back past his right ear, bending the bow deeply. Hard cut to side angle as the arrow leaves the string. Impact explodes in slow motion as the point drives into the first cat's face: glazed porcelain splits into sharp fragments, red and gold paint chips flying out with the white dust. Time locks at peak penetration. Camera orbits the frozen arrow buried in the broken figurine for one second. Quick insert ECU of the point breaking out through the back.

4-6s: [Dynamic Side Tracking] Action resumes. The first figurine is already shattered, only its base left on the stand. The master nocks a second arrow and draws in the same form. Slow-motion again: the second figurine tears apart sideways, large red and gold pieces scattering with the dust. Freeze at maximum destruction. Two rapid cuts: one over-shoulder from behind the master, one low angle under the flying fragments. Orbit holds half a second on the locked impact.

6-8s: [Handheld Close Action] The first two figurines are already broken, only bases and scattered shards remain. Third shot — short, vicious release. Slow-mo impact, the porcelain erupts into dozens of pieces, an ear and a raised paw flying. Full freeze. Camera pushes in and circles the frozen chaos for one beat. Quick insert of the master's focused face after the release, then back to the locked arrow.

8-10s: [Series of Fast Cuts] Time resumes. The master holds the bow upright and turns it in the window light. Tight ECU along the string: two strands lifted and frayed. Cut to his bare fingertips rubbing that spot, glove off. Cut to all three stands still shedding dust, every figurine shattered. Cut to the young woman's tense expression as she watches.

10-12s: [Medium Close-Up] The master rests the bow tip on the floor and looks straight at her. Calm, professional: "There are two strands frayed on the string… but your bow, ma'am… it goes through." The three stands behind them remain shattered. Hard cut to her face — a small, controlled smile of pride breaks through.

12-15s: [Final ECU Montage] Extreme close-up of the frayed but still taut string in the window light. Cut to porcelain fragments scattered across the wooden floor. Final frame holds on the young bowyer's quiet satisfaction as the master nods once.

Realistic TV broadcast quality, detailed porcelain shatter physics, sharp arrowhead and string detail, natural motion blur only on moving elements, stable characters, coherent impact timing, no artifacts.
```

### One uninterrupted FPV shot carries the viewer from the hidden entrance of Z…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
One uninterrupted FPV shot carries the viewer from the hidden entrance of Z. Thrillington’s private recording studio, through an elite lounge and instrument gallery, into the live room and control room, ending on his whispered philosophy. No cuts or broken continuity. It feels like one impossible breath of mystery, luxury, rhythm, and controlled creative chaos. Humid nighttime city street. A matte-black facade bears a glowing minimalist Z emblem. Stylish musicians, producers, artists, and invited guests gather as black luxury cars arrive. Understated security and poised hosts guide arrivals. Camera flashes reflect across wet pavement, jewelry, chrome, and tinted windows, establishing an invitation-only destination. Continuous geography: exterior entrance → soundproof doors → private lounge → instrument gallery → studio corridor → live room → vocal booth → control room → mixing console → Z. Thrillington. Visual language: black marble, smoked oak, walnut, aged brass, leather, polished concrete, acoustic panels, reflective glass, amber practicals, crimson accents, analog equipment, vintage microphones, glowing VU meters, cables, haze, and drifting smoke. First-person FPV with prestige feature-film quality and micro-drone agility. 18mm anamorphic feel, shallow focus, natural focus breathing, motion blur, lens bloom, anamorphic streaks, halation, rich blacks, warm highlights, and 35mm grain. The lens passes inches from faces, instruments, microphones, cables, consoles, and hands without collisions. Never travel straight. Flow through curves, spirals, rolls, banks, Dutch angles, rises, and dips. Slip between arriving guests as the doors open, skim polished floors, arc around a grand piano, weave between musicians and vintage guitars, pass through warm haze, then bank hard through soundproof doors as they swing open. In the lounge, artists, producers, and musicians converse naturally. Assistants carry lyric sheets, vinyl, headphones, instruments, and cables. A guitarist tunes, a pianist tests a progression, and someone drops a needle onto vinyl as the camera banks past. Amber practicals and glowing equipment reflect across marble, brass, wood, steel, instruments, and glass. Crossing the soundproof doors instantly shifts the energy while remaining in the same shot. Drum kits, amplifiers, microphone stands, suspended cables, keyboards, spinning tape reels, analog consoles, waveform monitors, isolation booths, and relentless movement replace the quiet elegance. The camera becomes faster and tighter. Musicians and engineers work at peak intensity. Drumsticks strike, guitar strings vibrate, piano keys race, vocalists lean into microphones, engineers ride faders, tape reels spin, patch cables connect, and performers pivot around the camera. Cymbals narrowly miss the lens, guitars pass overhead, lyric sheets flutter, fingers race across synthesizers, and drumsticks cross above as the camera rolls beneath them. Stage lights flare through volumetric haze. Hyperreal textures reveal worn leather, lacquered wood, brushed metal, glowing tubes, coiled cables, chrome, condensation, and sweat. The camera rises above the mixing console and settles into a controlled push toward Z. Thrillington. While the studio remains frantic, he is completely composed, seated behind the console and listening with total concentration. His signature oversized sunglasses remain firmly in place, covering his eyes throughout. He never touches, adjusts, lifts, lowers, removes, exchanges, damages, or alters them. His eyes are never visible. He adjusts one fader, turns one EQ knob, tilts his head, and presses play. Background softens into kinetic bokeh. He briefly acknowledges the lens, then returns to the music. Barely above a whisper: “The song already exists... I just find it.” On the final word, Z. Thrillington rests one hand on the console as the finished song fills the room. The mix collapses to warm tape hiss, faint speaker hum, and one controlled exhale. The camera settles into an intimate macro of glowing VU meters, spinning tape reels, illuminated analog needles, and warm vacuum tubes. End suspended on sound moving through the machine. Sound builds from nightlife, vinyl crackle, piano, guitar, and muted bass into drums, synthesizers, vocal takes, talkback, tape machines, and layered performance, then drops near silence for Z. Thrillington’s line. One continuous shot only. No cuts, teleportation, layout changes, duplicated people, lighting inconsistencies, drone shadows, fisheye distortion, collisions, impossible geometry, or nightclub aesthetic. Z. Thrillington must match the supplied reference. His sunglasses are permanent and must never move or reveal his eyes. Movement remains physically believable while feeling impossibly fluid. Hyperreal photorealism, warm amber bloom, 35mm grain, anamorphic character, volumetric haze, polished reflections, and tactile analog detail.
```

### Super casual real smartphone home video footage, rooftop hangout in Los Angeles…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Super casual real smartphone home video footage, rooftop hangout in Los Angeles with palm trees and city skyline visible at sunset, natural mobile phone camera with slight authentic handheld shake, normal frame rate with smooth natural motion, rapidfire montage with constant quick jump cuts every 1-2 seconds like scrolling through phone memories, unpolished authentic phone recording of friends chatting, snacking and enjoying the view, pure raw home video feel, no cinematic polish or heavy effects.
Use the provided reference photo as the strict ONLY visual reference for the main woman. Maintain her exact appearance with zero deviation. Generate a mixed group of friends around her on the rooftop, LA skyline and palm trees visible in the sunset light.
0-2.5s: Shaky rapid cuts main woman laughing near the rooftop railing, sunset and palm trees behind her.
2.5-5s: Abrupt jump cuts close-up of her smiling with a drink, then friends passing snacks around.
5-7.5s: Fast shaky she chats animatedly, city skyline glowing in the background, friends laughing nearby.
7.5-10s: Quick cut close-up warm smile toward camera, then jump to her laughing with friends near the view.
10-12.5s: Abrupt edit group gathered around a small table, casual toast with cups, string lights starting to glow.
12.5-15s: Final rapid transition main woman relaxed among friends, soft smile, calm LA sunset memory ending with gentle phone sway.
Natural smartphone video quality, slight real handheld shake, smooth normal frame rate motion with no low fps or choppy artifacts, authentic casual interactions and physics, stable main character consistency, no pro stabilization or effects.
```

### Photorealistic storyboard sheet for a 15-second Sengoku-period battle sequence.

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Photorealistic storyboard sheet for a 15-second Sengoku-period battle sequence.
Purpose: keyframe reference sheet for image-to-video generation.
Style target: live-action Japanese historical epic. Not illustration, not
concept art — each panel looks like a real frame grabbed from a film.

SHEET LAYOUT
Horizontal 3:2 sheet, clean white background, generous white gutters.
Five horizontal 16:9 panels with thin black borders, arranged in two rows:
  Top row: three equal panels — 1, 2, 3
  Bottom row: two equal panels — 4, 5 — aligned left, with the remaining
  space on the right left blank white.
Label each panel in its top-left corner in small plain black sans-serif,
outside the image area:
"1 / 0-3s"  "2 / 3-6s"  "3 / 6-8.5s"  "4 / 8.5-11.5s"  "5 / 11.5-15s"

CORE RULE — the armies are MASS, not people.
Both armies read as surfaces: colour fields, textures, tides. Never resolve a
single individual face in any panel. Every figure is either far enough to be
a shape, or a backlit silhouette inside smoke.

SETTING (all panels)
A vast flat river plain of churned mud and dry grass, low hills far behind.
Dawn, overcast, a cold pale sun low on the horizon, ground mist burning off.
Air loaded with dust and gunsmoke.
RED ARMY: a dense carpet of conical jingasa helmets and red-lacquered body
armour, thousands of tall crimson back-banners standing upright like reeds,
long spears in ranks.
BLUE ARMY: the same density in dark indigo and black armour, thousands of
deep navy back-banners, the same forest of spears.
Banners carry only simple shapes — a white circle on red, a white diamond on
navy. No complex crests, no lettering.
Palette: mud brown, crimson, indigo, bone-white smoke, cold grey sky.
Desaturated, earthy, high contrast, heavy atmospheric haze, natural film
grain. No fantasy, no glow, no magic.

PANEL 1 (0-3s) — Extreme high aerial, looking almost straight down. Two huge
colour fields, one crimson and one indigo, advancing across the mud toward
each other. Each reads as a single rippling carpet of helmets and upright
banners. A narrowing strip of open ground between them. Dust rolling off both
fronts.

PANEL 2 (3-6s) — Camera at ankle height, just above the mud, inside the red
army's front rank. Running legs, straw sandals and armoured shins pounding
through wet mud, spray in the air. Spear shafts angled overhead, coming down
from vertical toward level. Ahead, the blue mass fills the frame as a dark
advancing wall. No faces — only legs, mud and shafts.

PANEL 3 (6-8.5s) — THE IMPACT. High aerial again. The crimson field and the
indigo field slam together. The seam between them buckles and scatters,
banners toppling in a wave along the line of contact, a ring of dust blasting
outward from the seam. This is the hero frame of the sequence.

PANEL 4 (8.5-11.5s) — Mid-height, inside the dust at the seam, everything
backlit by the low sun. Near-black silhouettes churning against glowing
smoke — spear shafts crossing and snapping, banners falling, shapes surging
and giving way. Nothing resolves into a person. Grit streaking the lens.

PANEL 5 (11.5-15s) — High aerial, pulled further back and higher. The seam is
now a long ragged wound across the plain, crimson and indigo bleeding into
each other along a churning line. Dust boiling up and spreading until the
armies are half swallowed by it.

CONSTRAINTS
Keep the same plain, the same light direction, the same two banner colours,
and the same palette across all five panels.
Each panel must look like a photographic film frame, fully rendered, not a
sketch or line drawing.
Avoid: any close-up of an individual face, resolved facial features, named
characters, horses, modern objects, fantasy glow, magic, clean unworn armour,
complex family crests, lettering on banners, arrows in flight, castles or
buildings, gore, ranks dissolving into blurred mush, red and blue mixed
before panel 3, extra text, subtitles, watermark, logo.
quality: high
```

### 📷参照画像の成人女性を主人公として使用する。顔立ち、髪型、髪色、体型、水着のデザインと色を全編で忠実に維持する。主人公は明確に成人の女性。別人化や複数人化を避け…

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
📷参照画像の成人女性を主人公として使用する。顔立ち、髪型、髪色、体型、水着のデザインと色を全編で忠実に維持する。主人公は明確に成人の女性。別人化や複数人化を避ける。30秒、16:9、超高品質な実写テレビ番組。日本の大型水上障害物競技特番のようなリアルな会場、観客席、実況席、競技タイマー、固定カメラ、クレーンカメラ、望遠カメラを使用する。映画的なスローモーションは一切使わず、通常速度のリアルなテレビ中継として描く。

0〜4秒
競技前の挑戦者紹介。明るい屋外会場のスタート地点で、主人公がカメラに向かって自信のある笑顔を見せる。カメラに向かって両手を振った後、胸を張り、片手を腰に当て、もう片方の手で小さくガッツポーズをするセクシーで堂々としたポーズ。上半身をやや斜めに向け、胸元のラインが印象的に見える構図。テレビ番組の挑戦者紹介らしい、正面からのミディアムショットと軽いローアングルをテンポよく切り替える。健康的で華やかな色気。実況が日本語で「今回の挑戦者はこちら！」と紹介する。主人公がカメラに向かって「絶対クリアします！」と言い、笑顔から真剣な表情へ切り替わる。

4〜7秒
会場全体のワイドショット。巨大な水上障害物コースと観客席を見せる。主人公がスタートラインに立ち、深呼吸して構える。スタートブザーと同時に走り出す。実況は「さあ、スタートしました！」。

7〜13秒
第一障害は水上に並んだ回転ローラー。主人公が勢いよく踏み込み、腕でバランスを取りながら連続して渡る。横方向の追跡カメラ、正面の固定カメラ、足元の短いアップをテレビ中継らしく切り替える。一度だけ軽く体勢を崩すが、すぐに持ち直して最後の足場へ着地する。実況は「おっと危ない、しかし耐えた！」。

13〜19秒
第二障害は複数の吊りロープ。主人公が走った勢いで最初のロープを両手でつかみ、身体を振って次のロープへ移る。腕への負荷、ロープの張力、着地時の衝撃、荒くなる呼吸をリアルに表現する。カメラは障害物全体が見える引きの映像を中心にし、短く真剣な表情を捉えたバストアップカットを入れる。

19〜25秒
第三障害は急角度の反り立つ壁。主人公が助走して壁を駆け上がるが、一度目は上端に届かず滑り落ちる。安全マットの上に着地し、すぐに立ち上がって二度目へ挑戦する。二度目はさらに強く踏み込み、上端を両手でつかんで必死によじ登る。カメラは壁全体を映す正面固定、頂上側からの俯瞰、苦しそうな表情の寄りを自然に切り替える。実況は「ここが最大の難関だ！」。

25〜30秒
主人公が最終の細い足場を全力で進み、最後の隙間を通常速度でジャンプしてゴール台へ着地する。よろめきながらも赤いゴールボタンを押す。ブザーと大歓声が鳴り、タイマーが残りわずかで停止する。主人公は肩で息をしながら笑顔になり、「やった！」と女性らしい仕草で小さくジャンプしながら喜ぶ。最後は主人公とコース全体を映すテレビ番組らしいワイドショットで終了する。

映像表現
本物のテレビ番組で撮影したような自然な露出、現実的な照明、放送用カメラ特有の映像、自然な被写界深度、現場音、歓声、実況、足音、着地音、水しぶき、荒い息遣いを再現する。競技の位置関係が常に分かる編集にする。全動作は通常速度。スローモーション、スピードランプ、時間停止は禁止。主人公紹介では胸元を印象的に見せる
```

17–24s: Medium shot. The rewind ends flawlessly. The cook is once again carrying the bowls and kettle steadily. The woman looks up and casually raises two fingers, softly calling the cook by name. 24–30s: Extreme close-up. Hard cut to the woman's face. She takes one final slow slurp with a knowing smile, her eyes almost completely closed. Very shallow depth of field. The red lanterns behind her melt into soft, warm bokeh.

画像1のデコトラのデザイン、装飾、形状、電飾、比率をそのまま使用する。
画像2の男性を運転手として使用する。

画像4の配色・素材・質感を、変形後のすべてのメカパーツと最終形態のチャイニーズドラゴンロボットへ反映する。

カメラは運転席の左前から男性を斜めに捉えている。

デコトラは画像3の山道を猛スピードで下っている。

男性は真剣な表情で荒々しくハンドルを操作し、体を揺らしながら力強く運転している。

男性は叫ぶ。

叫んだ直後、ダッシュボード中央の大きな変形ボタンを勢いよく押す。

ヘッドライトは左右へ展開し、ドラゴンの発光する両目へ変形する。

フロントグリルは何重もの装甲へ展開し、ドラゴンの口を形成する。

フロントバンパーは巨大な下あごへ変形する。

ルーフ上の電飾は後方へ伸び、長い首を形成する。

荷台は数千もの細かなメカパーツへ分解され、胴体、肩、腕、尻尾へ精密に変形していく。

シャーシは伸縮し、内部から次々とメカフレームが展開する。

メッキパーツやマフラーは腕や尻尾の機械構造へ変形する。

龍の装飾や外装は何枚もの装甲へ分離し、完成したドラゴンロボットの全身を覆う。

変形中は無数の装甲、ギア、ピストン、リンク、レール、ロック機構が絶え間なく動き続け、超高密度なメカ変形が続く。

変形の途中、男性は運転席上部から外へ飛び出し、変形途中の頭部に片足を乗せ、両腕を組み、前を見据えながら堂々と立っている。

変形が完了すると、巨大なチャイニーズドラゴンロボットが完成する。

完成したロボットは画像4の配色や質感を忠実に受け継いでいる。

ドラゴンの両目が強く発光し、大きく咆哮する。

そのまま道路から力強く飛び立つ。

飛び立つ瞬間、前方には画像3と同じ山道の急カーブが見えており、ドラゴンはそのカーブへ進入する直前で道路を離れ、空へ向かって勢いよく舞い上がる。

すべての変形は実際の機械構造によって行われ、魔法のようなモーフィングは一切使用しない。

超高密度なメカニカル変形。
重量感のある金属表現。
実写映画品質。
車体と人物の形状は終始安定している。

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Style: Playful cinematic stop motion, slightly choppy, exaggerated physical comedy
Output frame rate: 24 frames per second, but animate the character with a deliberate 5 frames-per-second stop-motion rhythm
CHARACTER IDENTITY LOCK
The man in every scene is the same man from reference image 1: tall and slim, lean build, short dark hair, round red glasses that never leave his face, black t-shirt, black jeans, white sneakers. His eyes are completely natural, symmetrical human eyes with normal pupils; the red glasses have thin clear lenses that do NOT magnify, bend, distort or blur his eyes in any frame. Stable facial identity and consistent body proportions across all posts.
REFERENCE IMAGES (in upload order)
Bedroom post: reference image 1 (man asleep in bed Instagram post)
Coffee post: reference image 2 (espresso machine kitchen Instagram post)
Studio post: reference image 3 (creative studio with monitors Instagram post)
AI generation post: reference image 4 (giant screen with GENERAR button Instagram post)
Commercial set post: reference image 5 (empty cinematic spotlight set Instagram post)
Final commercial frame: reference image 6 (the man frozen in hero pose in the spotlight)
Create one continuous vertical Instagram feed where the same man secretly moves between different Instagram posts.
The entire 9:16 frame is a single continuous Instagram feed. Do not treat each reference image as a separate scene or separate shot.
Only the photo area inside each Instagram post becomes alive. The black Instagram headers, captions, icons, usernames, likes and comments remain visible and behave like fixed foreground interface layers.
The character must always remain behind the Instagram interface. He must never overlap the caption, icons, usernames or engagement area.
CRITICAL SWIPE TRANSITION ENGINE
Every transition must visibly look like the Instagram feed is being swiped to the next post.
Do not cut directly from one completed post to another completed post.
For every transition:
The man jumps downward inside the current post.
His body reaches the bottom edge of the photo area.
The black caption section passes in front of him and hides him.
While he is hidden behind the black interface, the entire Instagram feed physically slides upward in one continuous movement.
The current post moves upward and partially leaves the screen.
The next post rises into view from the bottom.
For several frames, both the outgoing post and incoming post must be visible together in the same 9:16 frame.
The man immediately continues the same downward movement, reappearing from behind the upper black edge of the incoming post.
He lands inside the next photo area without resetting his pose or speed.
Do not show a finger.
Maintain the same screen-space horizontal position of the man throughout the jump. His downward momentum before disappearing must match his downward momentum when he reappears.
Each swipe should last approximately 0.35 to 0.5 seconds.
During every swipe: no hard cut, no sudden replacement, no crossfade, no dissolve, no flash frame, no black frame, no full-screen blur, no teleportation, no camera repositioning, no instant background change, no separate establishing shot of the next post.
The Instagram interface, outgoing post and incoming post must all move together as one long vertically stacked feed.
Use slight vertical motion blur only on the moving feed during the fastest part of the swipe. The character and interface must remain readable.
0.0 TO 2.5 SECONDS
POST 1: THE SLEEPING CREATOR WAKES
Start with: reference image 1
Hold the post completely still for 0.3 seconds so it appears to be an ordinary Instagram image.
The sleeping man's eyes open behind his red glasses. He blinks.
His eyebrows rise: an idea has arrived.
He sits up suddenly, pushes off the duvet, grabs the notebook from the nightstand and tucks it under his arm.
He tiptoes toward the bottom edge of the photo with clear stop-motion poses and brief holds.
He crouches deeply and jumps straight downward from inside the photo. He does not jump toward the camera.
As he crosses the bottom edge, the black Instagram caption section moves in front of him and progressively hides his body.
At the same time, begin the Instagram swipe. The bedroom post slides upward, the coffee post rises from the bottom, both briefly visible together.
He continues falling and reappears from behind the coffee post's upper black boundary.
Sound effects: duvet rustle, floor creak, spring compression, continuous vertical swipe sound
2.5 TO 4.7 SECONDS
POST 2: THE COFFEE STOP
Reveal: reference image 2
The kitchen remains empty of other people.
The man lands on the kitchen counter beside the espresso machine. Cups and plates rattle.
He grabs the freshly poured espresso cup and takes one large exaggerated gulp. Steam puffs.
His eyes widen with energy, he does a satisfied full-body shake, now fully awake.
He sets the cup down, crawls quickly to the counter edge and performs another downward jump.
The black caption panel hides his body while the coffee post slides upward and the studio post rises from the bottom, both visible together.
He reappears from behind the studio post's upper black edge and continues falling without pause.
Sound effects: ceramic clink, espresso hiss, huge gulp, energized exhale, continuous vertical swipe
4.7 TO 7.3 SECONDS
POST 3: THE CREATION FRENZY
Reveal: reference image 3
The man lands seated directly into the rolling ergonomic chair, which rolls fast across the studio.
He plants his feet, scoots the chair to the desk and types furiously in exaggerated stop-motion bursts.
The monitors flicker with storyboards and video timelines. Sticky notes flutter off the wall. He scribbles one storyboard frame, holds it up, gives a satisfied nod.
He pushes off the desk, the chair spins once, and he leaps from the spinning chair diving down through the bottom edge of the photo.
The black footer hides him while the studio post slides upward and the AI generation post rises from the bottom, both visible together.
He reappears from behind the incoming post's upper black edge, continuing the same headfirst fall.
Sound effects: chair wheels rolling, frantic keyboard clacks, paper flutter, chair spin whoosh, continuous vertical swipe
7.3 TO 10.4 SECONDS
POST 4: THE GENERATE CLICK
Reveal: reference image 4
The man lands in front of the giant wall screen and looks up at the huge glowing GENERAR button.
One dramatic held beat.
He slaps the button with his open palm.
The interface floods with light: a progress bar races across the screen and the preview frame flashes bright.
Light and wind from the screen blow his clothes and hair back. He shields his eyes, grins, steps back and dives down through the bottom edge of the photo while the glow intensifies.
The interface hides his body while the AI post slides upward and the commercial set post rises from the bottom, both visible together.
He reappears from behind the commercial post's upper black boundary, still falling.
Sound effects: deep UI click, rising electronic hum, whoosh of light, continuous vertical swipe
10.4 TO 15.0 SECONDS
POST 5: INSIDE HIS OWN COMMERCIAL
Reveal: reference image 5
The set is empty when he arrives. No crew, no people.
The man falls into the cinematic set and lands inside the spotlight beam. Haze swirls around him.
He stands, adjusts his black t-shirt, and walks to the center mark leaving the haze curling behind him.
The camera performs a smooth continuous forward push toward him. No cut.
He plants his feet and straightens into a confident hero pose.
CRASH ZOOM: a rapid continuous camera push within the same unbroken shot. Do not hide a cut inside the crash zoom. No blur frame, no flash, no different image at the zoom peak.
During the crash zoom the lighting perfects itself, the teal and amber commercial grade settles, and all of his movement gradually stops.
He freezes in the final hero pose. End matching: reference image 6.
The final frame must emerge from the exact same continuous shot. No cut to the reference image, no instant replacement.
At the end he remains frozen as the finished commercial frame. Only his eyes briefly glance sideways, then return to stillness.
Sound effects: spotlight buzz, footsteps on glossy floor, cinematic braam, crash-zoom impact, sudden silence
MOTION STYLE
Use deliberate stop-motion movement: choppy pose-to-pose animation, brief held reaction frames, strong anticipation before jumps, squash and stretch during landings, exaggerated scrambling, fast but readable actions, slightly reduced movement sampling, minimal motion blur outside the feed swipes, stable facial identity, consistent body proportions.
The man's round red glasses stay on his face in every scene, and his eyes always stay natural, sharp and undistorted behind the lenses.
STRICT NEGATIVE INSTRUCTIONS
Do not hard-cut between posts.
Do not show a completed empty post followed by a completed new post.
Do not make the incoming post appear instantly.
Do not zoom into a post to hide the transition.
Do not fade between reference images.
Do not teleport the man.
Do not reset his falling pose between posts.
Do not let him overlap captions, icons or usernames.
Do not distort, warp, cross or enlarge his eyes at any moment.
Do not create any magical portal, glow ring or ripple when he enters the commercial set.
Do not cut during the crash zoom.
The complete video must feel like one long Instagram feed physically scrolling upward while the man continuously falls downward through each post.
```

### 【基本仕様】

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
【基本仕様】
アスペクト比16:9、総尺30秒、実写シネマティック、全編三人称。薄暗い地下鉄駅構内の連続した一つの銃撃戦を、常に移動しながら11ショットで途切れなく描く。

【被写体の固定】
主人公はImage1に写る女性ただ1人。容姿・髪型・服装・体型はすべてImage1に完全準拠させ、全編で同一人物を維持する。主人公が両手に構える二丁の拳銃はImage2に完全準拠させ、彫刻・光沢・銃身の長さを全編で変えない。敵はImage3に写る黒い戦術装備の特殊部隊員で、人数は15人。全員がヘルメットとゴーグルで顔が隠れ、装備・シルエットを全編で維持する。Image1とImage3にいない人物を追加しない。

【カメラの原則】
終始三人称。「戦闘を撮っているカメラ」として存在する。
広角の追従トラッキング（手持ちの微細な揺れ）＝ショット1・3・5・9／床スレスレの超ローアングル据え（高さ10cm）＝2・11／柱越しの望遠圧縮＝4・6／引きの俯瞰＝7／被写体を中心に横へ回り込むアークショット＝8・10。特定の身体部位に寄らない。捉えるのは全身の運動、銃口の火、暗闇と光。

【速度の設計（全編共通）】
基本は等速。速度が変わるのは以下だけで、切り替えは1フレームで瞬間的に行い、緩やかな加減速を挟まない。
バレットタイム＝銃弾・薬莢・埃・落下物だけが空中で完全に静止し、主人公だけが等速で動く（ショット2・6）。
超高速移動＝主人公だけが1フレームで画面から消え、埃の残像と風圧だけが残り、次の瞬間に別の場所に立っている。周囲の敵・埃・薬莢は等速のまま（ショット3・10）。

【身体の物理（全編共通）】
被写体を質量のあるものとして描く。着地と滑走では体重が乗って全身が一度沈み、遅れて跳ね返る。方向転換のたび衣が遅れて振れ戻る。発砲のたび銃口炎が空気中の埃を吹き飛ばし、反動が手首から肩へ抜ける。倒れる敵は装備の重さで重く落ち、床で一度跳ねる。薬莢は回転しながら落ち、濡れた床で跳ねて転がる。舞い上がった埃は光線の中を遅れて降りてくる。

【舞台】
夜の地下鉄駅。広いコンコースと島式ホーム。天井の蛍光灯は半分が切れて不規則に明滅し、壁際の非常灯の赤が点滅する。空気は硝煙と埃で白く濁り、光線が幾筋も可視化される。濡れたタイル床が全ての光を反射する。太い角柱が等間隔に並び、券売機、ベンチ、黒いトンネル口、線路。銃火のたび空間全体がストロボのように明滅する。

【ショット1／0.0〜2.0秒】
広角の追従トラッキング。1フレーム目から交戦の真っ最中。奥の暗闇で15の銃口が一斉に光り、曳光が主人公へ収束する。主人公は走らず、両腕を下げたまま一定の歩幅で歩いて前進する。跳弾が床と柱で火花を散らし、衣の裾だけが風圧で揺れる。テンポは速く、間を作らない。音は途切れない連射音の壁、薬莢が床を叩く音、跳弾の高い金属音。
日本語のセリフ：「目標確認、撃てェ!」（敵の指揮官／無線越しに割れた怒鳴り声）

【ショット2／2.0〜4.0秒】
床スレスレの超ローアングル、据え。バレットタイムに入る。銃弾と薬莢と埃が空中で完全に静止する。主人公だけが等速で上体を倒し、静止した弾列の間を縫って歩き抜ける。弾がすれ違うたび髪と衣が触れて揺れ、通過した軌跡の上だけ埃が渦を巻く。抜けた瞬間に等速へ戻り、全ての弾が背後の壁に一斉着弾してタイルの破片が飛ぶ。音は半分こもり、低い唸りと心音だけが残る。等速復帰と同時に着弾音が一気に戻る。

【ショット3／4.0〜6.6秒】
広角の追従トラッキング。敵1人が構えた銃口の先で、主人公が1フレームで消える。埃の残像だけが尾を引いて横へ流れる。敵は銃口を空へ振り回し、慌てて周囲を見回す。銃声が一瞬途切れ、明滅する蛍光灯のノイズが表に出る。
日本語のセリフ：「消えた!? どこだ——」（敵／上ずった早口、語尾が途切れる）

【ショット4／6.6〜9.2秒】
柱越しの望遠圧縮。主人公はすでにその敵の真後ろに立っている。両腕を左右へ大きく伸ばし、敵の両肩越しに二丁を同時発砲する。左右にいた敵が同時に吹き飛ぶ。振り返った目の前の敵の顎を、銃身を握った拳で下から打ち抜く。背景の柱の列が大きく圧縮されてボケる。音は二発の銃声が重なり、遅れて鈍い打撃音。

【ショット5／9.2〜12.0秒】
広角の追従トラッキング。柱の間を縫って移動しながら、左右へ交互に発砲する。濡れた床へ低く滑り込み、膝で滑走しながら二丁を左右へ振り分けて撃つ。滑走で体が一度沈み、止まりぎわに遅れて上体が起きる。薬莢が回転して床に落ち、跳ねて転がる。音は滑走の摩擦音、連続する銃声、水を弾く音。
日本語のセリフ：「囲めッ! 逃がすな!」（敵／短く鋭い怒号）

【ショット6／12.0〜15.0秒】
柱越しの望遠圧縮。上のエスカレーターと階段から敵が挟撃する。主人公は柱を蹴って壁を三歩走り、空中で反転する。反転の頂点で0.5秒だけバレットタイムに入り、静止した弾幕の中で二丁の照準だけが上下の敵へ同時に向く。等速へ戻り、上下の敵が同時に落ちる。音は反転の瞬間だけ無音に近づき、等速復帰で銃声が一気に戻る。

【ショット7／15.0〜18.5秒】
引きの俯瞰、据え。12人が床に倒れ、硝煙が層になって漂う。主人公
```

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
ART STYLE
Strictly follow this 2D animation style: hand-painted digital gouache with visible dry-brush grain and unclosed edges, zero outlines — form defined entirely by flat blocked color. Chunky geometric shape design with extreme size contrast between characters. Limited high-chroma palette on large flat value masses. Backgrounds reduced to silhouetted notan planes with silhouetted foreground framing. Action beats drop the environment entirely for a single saturated color field. Highly cinematic 2D animation.

REFERENCES
Environment: environment.png
Character consistency — Gorlock: gorlock-sheet.png
Character consistency — Turan: turan-sheet.png

SCENE
Two mountain-scale monsters fight a chaotic, majestic, epic, explosive, huge-impact battle in the environment above. Opens mid-action on a close-up of Gorlock roaring, furious and enraged. Turan comes down as a living inferno bullet, crashing into the ground where Gorlock stands. They charge into each other immediately and fight with physical attacks, elemental force and magic at massive scale impact range.

CAMERA & PACING
24 different, distinct camera angles, none repeated. An epic, non-stop fight — never stationary, fast-paced, with strong build-up pacing and clear key moments. High-speed motion and movement from both characters throughout.

MOTION & PHYSICS
Weight before speed — every charge starts with a heavy compressed crouch, then explosive release. Anticipation held two beats longer than natural, impact snapped to a single frame, recovery slowed by drag. Mass follows through: mane, fire-fur and smoke lag half a beat behind the body, then overshoot and settle. Ground reacts before the body lands — dust rings and flattening grass telegraph each footfall. Impacts push through, never stopping at contact: bodies deform on collision, slide, and dig trenches. Camera shake scales to impact size, settling with inertia rather than snapping back. Speed ramps: slow on wind-up, real-time on approach, brief hold at contact, fast on the throw-through. Debris travels as flat chunky shards on arcing parabolic paths, tumbling as it falls.

SFX
Deep sub-bass roar with a gravel throat rasp. Low-frequency ground rumble under every footfall. Tearing crackle of open flame. Sharp crystalline chime on each energy discharge. Whip-crack air displacement on the charge. Granular soil and rock crumble on landing. Distant echo rolling back off the far hills. Low sustained wind bed between hits.

VFX
Hard-edged white shockwave discs on every impact. Flat radial dust rings expanding outward. Brush-textured ember showers rising in slow drift. Chunky flat debris shards. Green energy lances with spiked hard-edge silhouettes. Violet smoke ribbons in flat blocked masses. Screen-wide saturated color-field flashes at peak contact. Heat shimmer rendered as offset dry-brush strokes. Dirt streaks flicking across the lens.

ENGAGEMENT RULE (strict, entire video)
Gorlock and Turan always face each other and always move toward each other. They never turn away, never retreat, never flee, and never run in the same direction or side by side. Every displacement is either a charge into the opponent or being driven backward by the opponent while still facing them — their motion vectors are permanently opposed and head-on from the first frame to the last.

ENDING
The video ends still in the middle of the action. Both are still fighting, neither has shown any sign of defeat, and the battle is far from over.
```

→ (5s, final shot) Sets the card on the windowsill, reaches toward camera. "See you next batch." Hand covers lens as recording ends.

30-Second Continuous Sequence

A gigantic greasy burger monster erupts from underground, built from enormous beef patties, dripping cheese, lettuce, onions and sesame buns. Melted cheese stretches everywhere as it rises several stories high.

She wakes naturally, opens the wooden window, and breathes in the fresh countryside air while looking across mist-covered fields. 30秒2.5D动画电影质感，16:9，4K，24fps。从第0秒开战，全程双方同时进攻、互不相让，无任何停顿、喘息、蓄力空档或空镜。
场景：正午强光下的巨型白色盐湖平原，地表覆盖一层薄如镜面的浅水，完整倒映蓝天与白云，远处可见白色石柱遗迹与山脉，天空湛蓝、阳光刺目。

【格斗设计·角色A】精准剑术＋黄金操控：居合拔刀、突刺、横薙、逆手换握、刀镡卸力；液态黄金随刀流动，可瞬间凝固成金墙、金锁链、悬浮金刃阵与可踩踏的金阶；以最小动作化解最大攻击，善用支点与反射，打法克制冷静。
【格斗设计·角色B】重械压制流：巨斧大开大合，抡、砸、扫、投掷；不闪避而是硬吃并反击，靠体重与惯性碾压；以岩浆喷射作为推进器完成加速冲锋与空中姿态修正；斧刃甩出岩浆弧，砸地引发岩浆柱。
【招式规则】全片不出现两次相同的攻击方式；主动权每2秒易手一次；每2秒变换交战距离与高度层次——贴身缠斗／中距对械／远程放招／空中／贴水面／高空；穿插虚招、夺械、反制与借力，不是纯粹硬碰硬。
【身体表演】每次受力都有真实反应：滑步卸力、踉跄修正、重心下沉、肩背卸劲、呼吸起伏；battle damage持续累积——衣物撕裂、皮肤擦痕、焦痕、发丝散乱随时间递增，绝不复原。
【对抗密度】每2秒至少完成4至6次攻防交换，画面里永远有两个人在动。
【规模基准】所有特效按公里级呈现，金色结构与岩浆脉络延伸至地平线，每次大招都改变天空颜色与云的形状。
【运镜原则】每2秒换一次机位，相邻机位的角度、焦段与运动方向必须完全不同；大量使用极端仰拍、俯冲航拍、荷兰角、穿越镜头、急速变焦与速度斜坡。

【角色A灵气】流动的液态黄金环绕全身，金箔与金粉持续飞散，刀身上的黄金不断滴落并在空中凝固成刃；金面反射正午阳光形成刺目光斑。
【角色B灵气】暗红熔光自体内透出，高温辐射把周围空气强烈扭曲，黑色浮渣与橙色火星持续剥落，脚下盐壳一踩即焦。
【魔法物理】黄金：液态时有真实重量与黏稠拉丝，凝固瞬间泛起金属涟漪，受击碎裂成金片飞散。岩浆：亮橙内芯配暗红结壳，接触浅水瞬间爆发白色蒸汽与爆鸣，冷却后凝成黑曜石与尖锐浮渣，滴落处烧出焦黑坑洞。
【破坏累积】0秒完整镜面盐湖倒映蓝天；8秒水面被震出巨型环形波、局部沸腾冒蒸汽、盐壳龟裂；16秒半个湖面被蒸干成焦黑盐壳、蒸汽云柱冲天、金色碎片洒满地面；24秒大片黑曜石凝成尖峰、金色巨构横贯天空、蒸汽遮日；30秒整片盐湖化为黑曜石荒原，金色残刃插满地面，蒸汽与金粉漫天飘散。

0-2秒｜极低角度贴水面仰拍急速推近：角色B自高空抡斧砸落，角色A以液态黄金瞬间凝成的金墙硬接；接触点爆发白色蒸汽与金花，湖面被震出直径数百米的环形波，蓝天倒影整片碎裂。
2-4秒｜贴身缠斗，手持感肩上跟拍：B连续横抡巨斧逼A后退，A以刀镡卸力并贴着斧面滑步切入；液态黄金顺斧柄逆流缠住B手腕试图夺械，B以掌心岩浆瞬间烧断金链。
4-6秒｜远程压制，360度快速环绕轨道：A挥刀甩出数十道悬浮金刃在空中列阵齐射；B旋身以斧面格挡，接住的金刃在斧刃高温下熔成岩浆被她反甩回去，熔金滴落处炸起蒸汽。
6-8秒｜垂直层次，摇臂垂直上升接俯拍：B把斧砸进湖底引发岩浆柱冲天贯穿水面，A踩着自己凝固的金阶螺旋上行躲避；蒸汽云柱在两人之间轰然升起，主动权转到A手中。
8-10秒｜空中近战，盘旋跟随、0.5倍慢动作后瞬间恢复常速：半空四次高速交锋，A以悬浮金刃作支点连续变向短促突刺，B在空中抡斧并靠背后岩浆喷射修正姿态硬接。
10-12秒｜交错换位，一镜穿越接180度甩回：两人同一瞬间对冲擦身而过，镜头从刀锋与斧刃交错的缝隙高速穿过再甩回；两人背向而立、逆光剪影，金粉与橙色火星逆向对流。

日本のフルカラーアニメ映画、シネマティック高品質映像。
BGMなし、セリフなし、字幕なし、文字なし、環境音あり。
フルサイズ換算24mm F1.4の広角レンズで撮ったような浅い被写界深度とボケ感。
手前の前ボケは大きく柔らかく、背景は自然に溶ける。

# 制約
すべてのカットで画角・アングル・焦点距離を変えて単調にしない。
各カットの角括弧内の数値は、そのカットの開始時刻と終了時刻を秒で示す。
この時刻配分に従って全カットを描き、最後のカットまで到達すること。
姿勢の定義：彼女は全カットを通してキッチンに立って調理している。
屈む動作、座る動作、部屋を出る動作は映さない。
配置の定義：シンクは画面左、コンロは画面右。作業は画面左から右へ移っていく。
カメラは常にカウンターの手前側にあり、この左右関係を全カットで維持すること。
光の定義：唯一の光源は暖かいアンバーの室内灯。
光の状態は全カット通して一定に保ち、時間帯は変えない。
奥行きの定義：すべてのカットで前景・中景・遠景を明確に分離する。
前景には湯気・水の筋・カウンターの縁などを大きくボカして配置し、
被写体を手前から覗き込むような奥行きを作る。
パララックスの定義：カメラが移動するカットでは、
前景を最も速く、中景を中程度、遠景を最も遅く動かし、三層の速度差を明確につけること。

# 登場人物
## 女性
成人女性。
ショートパンツ、裸足、黒いネイル。
人に見せることを意識していない、深夜の部屋着姿。

# 情景
深夜のモダンなキッチン。大きな窓越しに夜景が広がる。
暖かいアンバーの室内灯だけが空間を照らし、
光と影が肌の輪郭をやわらかく浮き上がらせる。
調理の湯気と熱気が空気をわずかに揺らしている。
誰にも見られていない、静かで親密な深夜。
小物の定義：アサリは大量にあり、ステンレスの大きなボウルの中で
底一面に幾重にも重なって水に沈んでいる。
オリーブオイルは細長い透明なガラス瓶に入っており、
口には細い金属の注ぎ口が差してある。傾けて注ぐ。
白ワインは濃い緑のガラス瓶で、ラベルには文字が入っていない。
刻んだパセリはカウンターの小皿に用意されている。
皿は白い浅いパスタ皿が1枚だけ。
奥行き構成：前景に湯気とカウンターの縁、中景に彼女と調理器具、遠景に窓の夜景。

# シーン
深夜、女性が一人でボンゴレを作る。
貝を洗うところから盛り付けまで、料理の手順と彼女の仕草を交互に見せる。
湯気・貝の艶・素肌・何気ない動作が自然にシーンに溶け込んでいく。

cut1: [0.0-1.9]
ワイドショット、斜め後方から。カメラが画面左へゆっくり横移動する。
画面左のシンクの前に立つ彼女の後ろ姿。流水が細く落ちている。
手前を湯気の立つ大鍋の輪郭が大きくボケて速く横切り、
中景の彼女はゆっくりと相対的に流れ、
遠景の窓の夜景はほとんど動かない。三層の速度差を明確につける。
室内灯が背中を暖かく縁取っている。
環境音：流水の音、遠い街のざわめき。

cut2: [1.9-3.2]
クローズアップ、正面のやや上から見下ろす。
流水の下、ネイルを施した両手が山盛りのアサリを掻き混ぜている。
指の間で無数の黒い殻がこすれ合い、表面から細かい砂が流れ落ちていく。
シンクの中にはまだ大量のアサリが積み重なったままになっている。
指先が冷水でわずかに赤らんでいる。
環境音：貝殻がこすれ合う連続した音、水がステンレスを叩く音。

cut3: [3.2-5.4]
クローズアップ、正面のローアングル。
あおりの構図で、前屈みになった上体を下から見上げる。
手前で流水の筋が大きくボケて画面の右端を縦に横切っている。
紺色のくせ毛が重力で前に垂れている。
目線は手元の水に落ち、表情はクールで集中している。
前屈みの姿勢で襟元の布が体から離れて前に垂れ、
鎖骨から胸元へ続くラインが自然に見える。
跳ねた冷水の粒が首から鎖骨に散り、
一粒が鎖骨のくぼみをゆっくりと滑り落ちていく。
水面の反射光が下から顎と首をゆらゆらと照らす。
環境音：流水の音。

cut4: [5.4-6.4]
マクロ、斜め上から。
木のまな板。ネイルを施した指がにんにくを押さえ、
包丁が薄い断面を次々と落としていく。
にんにくの汁が刃に薄く光る。
手前にまな板の木目の縁が大きくボケて入る。
環境音：包丁がまな板を叩くリズミカルな音。

cut5: [6.4-7.6]
クローズアップ、湯面すれすれの水平から。
大きな鍋が激しく沸騰している。
パスタが束になって湯の中へ落ち、白い泡と湯気が勢いよく立ち上がる。
手前を立ちのぼる湯気が厚くボケて流れる。
環境音：激しい沸騰音、パスタが湯に入る音。

cut6: [7.6-8.6]
マクロ、フライパンの縁スレスレの真横から。
まだ冷たいオリーブオイルの中に、にんにくの薄切りと赤唐辛子が沈んでいる。
オイルがゆっくりと温まり、にんにくの縁から細かい泡が立ちはじめる。
環境音：オイルが温まりはじめる微かな音。

cut7: [8.6-9.8]
クローズアップ、右斜め上から。
熱したフライパンの上でボウルが大きく傾けられ、
大量のアサリが一斉に滑り落ちていく。
無数の黒い殻が次々と熱した金属に当たって跳ね、フライパンを埋め尽くす。
油が細かく飛び散る。
手前を薄い湯気がボケて流れる。
環境音：貝が金属に当たる連続した硬い音、油が跳ねる音。

cut8: [9.8-11.2]
クローズアップ、真上からの俯瞰。
緑のガラス瓶の口だけがフレームに入り、ゆっくりと
```

### FORMAT: Vertical 9:16 smartphone video. 30 seconds of continuous handheld…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
FORMAT: Vertical 9:16 smartphone video. 30 seconds of continuous handheld footage. A real modern person walking through ancient Rome, 80 AD, filming on their phone. NOT a film. NOT cinematic. NOT color graded. Raw casual smartphone footage — looks like genuinely leaked impossible footage someone reposted.

CAMERA BEHAVIOR: Handheld, walking pace the entire time — camera bobs naturally with each step, micro-shaky, never stabilized. The filmer is exploring casually, phone half-raised, glancing the camera toward whatever catches their attention — never lingering more than a few seconds on anything. Autofocus hunts twice. Framing is instinctive and imperfect — tops of buildings get cut off, horizon tilts. One brief pixelated digital zoom. No deliberate composition ever.

VISUAL TEXTURE: Compressed digital smartphone video. Slightly overexposed Mediterranean sky. Natural noise in shadows. Flat, ungraded phone-camera color in harsh midday sun. Bleached stone, dusty ochre, terracotta. Noon light, no mood lighting.

SECONDS 0–5: Opens mid-stride, no intro. Walking through a packed Roman street market — vendors shouting in Latin, stalls of bread, olives, live chickens, amphorae. People brush past close to the lens. The camera sweeps across the chaos while walking, catching fragments — a merchant weighing coins, a woman haggling, a donkey. Keeps moving.

SECONDS 5–10: The filmer turns a corner and passes a slave market — a raised wooden platform, a trader calling out in Latin, a line of men and women standing while buyers inspect them. The camera catches it in passing — a few seconds, slightly too long, the way you’d film something disturbing without stopping — then the filmer keeps walking. Crowd noise continues, nobody around reacts, it’s an ordinary sight to them.

SECONDS 10–15: Shouting erupts ahead — two men are brawling outside a tavern, wrestling into a vegetable stall, produce scattering, the vendor screaming at them. A small crowd instantly forms, laughing and jeering. The camera swings toward it while still walking, gets a shaky half-view through the gathered onlookers’ shoulders, then the filmer pushes past and moves on. The shouting fades behind.

SECONDS 15–21: The street opens up. A column of Roman legionaries marches directly across the filmer’s path — red tunics, segmented armor, shields, hobnailed sandals hammering the stone in unison. The filmer stops walking for a moment to let them pass, camera panning along the line. Loud, rhythmic, intimidating. The last soldier passes and the filmer immediately walks on.

SECONDS 21–27: The filmer rounds a final corner and the Colosseum fills the frame — brand new, one year old, blinding white travertine, massive beyond the phone’s ability to capture it. Enormous crowds stream toward its arches. Colored awnings. Vendors selling food outside. The filmer tilts the phone up while walking toward it, trying to fit the full height — fails, cuts off the top. Tries the digital zoom on the upper arches — pixelates badly — pulls back out.

SECONDS 27–30: Still walking closer, camera on the arches and the crowds funneling in. A muffled roar erupts from inside the arena — tens of thousands of voices at once. The camera instinctively jerks up toward the sound. Cuts off abruptly mid-motion. No resolution.

AUDIO: Constant walking footsteps on stone. Wind buffeting the mic. Overlapping Latin everywhere — vendors, arguments, the slave trader’s calls, the brawl crowd jeering, marching boots in unison, and finally the massive interior roar of the Colosseum crowd. No music. No narration.

PEOPLE: Historically accurate. Plain wool tunics in undyed cream, brown, rust. Real weathered Mediterranean faces. No Hollywood Romans, no togas on commoners. Nobody performs for the camera. Everyone mid-task, mid-transaction, mid-argument. A real living city.
```

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
[SCENE] A 30-second stylised 3D animated short. A handmade clay mug at the back of a kitchen cupboard waits to be chosen, while the perfect porcelain cups at the front are used every day. Warm, funny, quietly moving. 2.35:1.

[REFERENCES] All three: reproduce 100%, appearance only, do not inherit the reference background, lighting or camera.

PIP: a small lopsided handmade clay mug, wobbly uneven walls, rim not level, small crooked handle, patchy cornflower-blue glaze thick at the base and thin at the rim showing terracotta beneath, a thumbprint in one side. Alive — two large expressive eyes with glassy highlights, small simple mouth. No arms, no legs.

CLIVE: a flawless white porcelain teacup with a fine gold band at rim and foot, perfectly symmetrical, thin-walled. Alive — two large expressive eyes, small refined mouth, serene and faintly superior. No arms, no legs.

the set — cupboard interior, light and atmosphere ONLY: sage-green shelves with chipped paint, a front row of gold-rimmed cups, a dusty gap at the back, door ajar with a warm shaft of morning sun and dust motes.

[TECHNICAL] High-end stylised 3D animated feature quality. Soft global illumination, physically based rendering, subsurface scattering in glaze and porcelain, warm colour science, shallow depth of field, volumetric light shaft. Beautiful CG animation — NOT photoreal, NOT live action, NOT stop-motion, NOT illustration, NOT anime.

ANIMATION LAW: performance is eyes, mouth and tilt only. Neither has arms or legs. They rock, lean and settle on the shelf — never walk, hop or fly.

[STRUCTURE] 13 segments, 12 hard cuts. S1/S13 = 84° wide inside the cupboard · characters = 47° at 30-60cm · reactions = 35° close · one 18° macro on the hand. No zooming.

[TIMELINE]

0:00-0:03 — 84° wide. Inside the dark cupboard. The front row of gold-rimmed cups gleams. Far at the back, in shadow, PIP sits alone.

0:03-0:05 — 35° close on PIP, eyes bright, leaning forward hopefully.

PIP: "Today's the day."

0:05-0:07 — 47°. CLIVE at the front of the shelf, not turning round.

CLIVE: "You said that yesterday."

0:07-0:09 — The cupboard door swings open. A shaft of morning light. Both squint. A hand reaches in and takes CLIVE from the front row.

0:09-0:11 — 35° close on PIP, light fading from his face as the door closes. His shoulders drop.

PIP: "Tomorrow, then."

0:11-0:16 — MONTAGE, fast cuts on the same 84° angle: the door opens and closes six times, light shifting from morning gold to grey to evening amber to blue. Each time a hand takes a cup from the front. Each time PIP leans forward hopefully and settles back.

0:16-0:18 — 35° close on PIP in the dark, eyes lowered, no longer leaning.

0:18-0:20 — 47°. The door opens again. Grey rainy light this time, slower.

0:20-0:22 — 18° macro. The hand comes in — and stops. It hovers over the front row. It doesn't take one.

0:22-0:25 — 47°. The hand moves past the front row, reaching into the shadow at the back, and closes gently around PIP.

0:25-0:27 — 35° close on CLIVE, eyes wide, entirely surprised.

CLIVE: "...Huh."

0:27-0:29 — 47°. PIP lifted out into the grey light, eyes enormous, overwhelmed.

PIP: "Told you."

0:29-0:30 — 84° wide from inside the cupboard, out through the open door: PIP on the counter in soft light, steam rising. Hold.

[PHYSICS] Ceramic weight — both rock and settle with real mass, wobbling on their bases, never bouncing lightly. The clay is duller and heavier than the porcelain, which rings faintly. The door swings with real hinge weight. Steam rises and curls. Dust drifts slowly in the shaft.

[LIGHTING] One warm shaft through the door as the only source, shifting colour through the montage. 60:30:10 — deep cupboard shadow 60% / the shaft 30% / accent gold rims and PIP's blue glaze 10%. The back is genuinely dark; PIP is barely lit until 0:22.

[AUDIO] MUSIC: warm score, solo piano with light strings — a simple hopeful theme entering at 0:03, thinning through the montage, dropping to almost nothing at 0:16, blooming warmly at 0:22 and holding. No comedy stings, no plucked cuteness.

SFX: door hinge and latch, ceramic settling on wood, the fine ring of porcelain, a distant kettle, rain on a window at 0:18, hot liquid pouring at 0:29.

DIALOGUE: five short lines only, warm and understated, never shouted, never cartoonish.

[LOCKS] Exactly two characters, both crockery — nothing else comes alive, no faces on any other object. No human is shown beyond a single hand and forearm — no face, no body. Neither character has arms, legs, or moves on its own. PIP is never repaired, never becomes perfect, never cruelly mocked. Nothing breaks, nothing is dropped. No text, labels, logos or watermarks. Never photoreal, never live action, never stop-motion.
```

In the garage it weaves through clutter and causes things to wobble or fall. On the rooftop it skids across surfaces and startles other animals. In the tree it shakes branches and scatters leaves. In the street or alley it darts through trash, shadows, and unexpected little messes, always leaving brief chaos behind before sprinting onward.The cat behaves like a restless, curious nighttime troublemaker: climbing, leaping, squeezing through gaps, chasing tiny movements, staring at strange things, swatting objects, knocking things over, and vanishing into corners before bursting back out again. Every scene should contain a clear action, reaction, or small disaster caused by the cat.Everything around it feels sleepy and still, with dark houses, quiet gardens, dim windows, silent rooftops, and empty streets. The contrast is that the cat is pure energy, moving so quickly that the world smears into motion blur and fragmented glimpses of chaos. The overall feeling is funny, lively, mischievous, and cinematic, like a whole secret night of cat behavior compressed into one rapid continuous chase.

### Use ChatGPT to reverse-engineer a reference chase clip into reusable Seedance…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use ChatGPT to reverse-engineer a reference chase clip into reusable Seedance 2.5 prompt blocks by requesting separate sections for Action, Camera, Speed, Composition, Environment, and Sound.  
Prompt high-speed scenes with explicit visual speed cues (start already at full speed, let the camera get overtaken, strong foreground parallax, vehicles rapidly change size in frame, vary vehicle distance, short interior inserts, reveal hazards late) instead of only using generic terms like high-speed chase.  
Keep physical action descriptions plain, literal, and easy to visualize, and put cinematic/technical detail into separate camera/speed/composition/atmosphere blocks.  
Audit and rewrite your Seedance 2.5 prompt by pasting your reference analysis alongside ByteDance’s official Seedance prompting guide and asking ChatGPT to simplify and adapt it into production-ready blocks.  
Separate action instructions from camera direction and give each generated clip one clear action arc to improve clarity and consistency.  
For complex sequences, generate multiple shorter shots and edit them together instead of attempting one continuous ~30-second generation to reduce mistakes and rerolls.  
Use Seedance 2.5 multimodal referencing with character/vehicle/environment sheets to preserve visual identity, keeping the total references to 7 or fewer at once.
```

### Seedanceでイベント告知PVを作るときは、告知画像を添付して「画像内のキャラ・UI・イベント名/属性/報酬などを読み取り、10秒PVの秒単位タイムライン＋…

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Seedanceでイベント告知PVを作るときは、告知画像を添付して「画像内のキャラ・UI・イベント名/属性/報酬などを読み取り、10秒PVの秒単位タイムライン＋カメラワーク＋モーション＋文字演出＋ネガティブプロンプトまで含む完成形プロンプトを日本語/英語で出力せよ」という指示書プロンプトを先に作り、その出力をSeedanceに投入する  
キャラクター同一性を保つために、添付画像の人物を同一人物として扱い、顔/髪/瞳/衣装/装飾/非人間要素/年齢印象を維持し、別人化・衣装/髪型/性別/年齢変更・群衆や他キャラの追加を禁止するネガティブ指定を入れる  
10秒PVは0.0–1.5秒導入→1.5–3.5秒キャラ登場→3.5–6.0秒不穏化→6.0–8.5秒決定的演出→8.5–10.0秒ロゴ/開催告知/報酬/参加ボタン風締め、の秒割り構成でプロンプト化する  
静止画を動画化するモーションは「髪がゆっくり揺れる」「翼が一度だけ重く羽ばたく」「鎖が微かに揺れる」「粒子/花弁/炎粉が流れる」「UIフレームが発光」「報酬カードがスライド表示」など“過剰に動かしすぎない”高級PV寄りの動きに限定して指定する  
文字は画像内テキストの完全再現を狙わず、視認できる情報をもとにPV用の短い刺さるテキスト（3〜5個）へ再構成する  
横長16:9のゲームイベントPV前提で、ズームイン/アウト、パララックス、手前UIと奥背景の奥行き、目線誘導、ラストのロゴ位置までカメラ指定を入れる  
Seedanceの安定性を上げるために尺を15秒に設定して生成する（文字が安定する可能性がある）
```

#### 💡 Dicas

### Seedance 2.5の公式プロンプトガイドURLをClaude Codeに貼って読み込ませ、ガイド内容を踏まえたSeedance用プロンプトをClaude…

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Seedance 2.5の公式プロンプトガイドURLをClaude Codeに貼って読み込ませ、ガイド内容を踏まえたSeedance用プロンプトをClaude Codeに書かせる  
Seedance 2.5のt2v生成はDreaminaで480p出力→Topazで4Kにアップスケールするワークフローにする  
Seedance 2.5のプロンプトはDuration/アスペクト比/fps（例: Duration: 10s | 9:16 | 24fps）を冒頭で明示する  
複数カット構成は[TIMESTAMP STORYBOARD]で0-2s/2-4s…のように秒数ごとに国・人物・台詞・動作を指定して破綻を減らす  
カット間の繋ぎは手のひらでレンズを覆うMASK TRANSITIONを指定し、hard cuts禁止・突然の出現禁止も併記して自然な遷移にする  
全カットでカメラ条件を固定（chest-up close-up、eye level、locked off、selfie distance、35mm、shallow depth of field）と明記して一貫性を出す  
肌の質感を保つためにno beauty filter/no skin smoothing/natural film grain/true pores/real skin textureを明示する  
リップシンク精度を上げるために各台詞を指定言語で書き、accurate native pronunciationとmatching lip movementを明示する  
不要要素を避けるためにNo subtitles/No on-screen text/No BGM/No watermark/Pure videoを明示し、禁止事項（例: jitter/flicker/同じ顔の再利用/2人同フレーム）を列挙する  
コストを抑える用途設計としてSeedance 2.5は15秒〜1分30秒程度の短尺CM向けに使う
```

#### 💡 Dicas

Seedance 2.5の公式プロンプトガイドURLをClaude Codeに貼って読み込ませ、ガイド内容を踏まえたSeedance用プロンプトをClaude Codeに書かせる

### Preserve character consistency by explicitly instructing Seedance to keep the…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Preserve character consistency by explicitly instructing Seedance to keep the exact identity and wardrobe and referencing character sheets (e.g., @target_character_sheet, @assassin_character_sheet).  
Lock vehicle appearance by using identity reference sheets for each vehicle (e.g., @landcruiser_sheet for the Toyota Land Cruiser, @kawasaki_ninja_sheet for the motorcycle).  
Ground environment and tone by attaching moodboards as references (e.g., @tokyo_street_mood_1990s, @chase_moodboard_1990s).  
Start the prompt “in the middle of the chase” with both vehicles already at full speed to maximize immediate perceived speed from frame one.  
Increase perceived speed by specifying low road-level angles, wheel-level inserts, front-quarter vehicle shots, over-the-shoulder pursuit views, compressed telephoto traffic shots, and a brief interior insert.  
Create dynamic camera energy by directing the camera to be repeatedly overtaken, fall behind, then rush past as the crash unfolds.  
Boost motion and depth by prompting close-to-lens foreground parallax (traffic/barriers/road markings/lights/water spray passing near the lens) and rapid scale changes.  
Maintain clear visual hierarchy by instructing the Land Cruiser to dominate the frame during the crash, then shifting emphasis to the assassin as she recovers control.  
End with a specific final shot instruction (front-facing tracking shot of the assassin riding toward camera with the explosion behind) to control the closing composition.  
Specify lighting/atmosphere cues (firelight, rain haze, neon spill, sodium streetlights, glossy wet-road reflections) to unify the look.  
Add “Cinematic anamorphic” to the prompt to push a widescreen, filmic chase aesthetic.  
Include “No music” to prevent the model from implying or adding musical elements.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

### Use Seedance 2.5 t2v with a JSON prompt that defines a title, a detailed style…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use Seedance 2.5 t2v with a JSON prompt that defines a title, a detailed style string (e.g., 3D Pixar family animation, pastel sky, golden morning light), and a shots array.  
Break the video into timestamped shots (e.g., 00:00-00:03, 00:03-00:06) and specify shot type per segment (EXTREME WIDE/WIDE/CLOSE-UP/MEDIUM/TRACKING) to control pacing and composition.  
For each shot, explicitly include action, camera movement/angle (e.g., crane descending, static, side-tracking fast, low angle, pull back), and dialogue/SFX to guide character beats and storytelling continuity.  
Add micro-visual detail in close-ups (e.g., macro, water droplets on freckles, vapor trails, sun flares, rainbows in vapor) to increase perceived realism and “alive” worldbuilding.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

Break the video into timestamped shots (e.g., 00:00-00:03, 00:03-00:06) and specify shot type per segment (EXTREME WIDE/WIDE/CLOSE-UP/MEDIUM/TRACKING) to control pacing and composition.  
For each shot, explicitly include action, camera movement/angle (e.g., crane descending, static, side-tracking fast, low angle, pull back), and dialogue/SFX to guide character beats and storytelling continuity.  
Add micro-visual detail in close-ups (e.g., macro, water droplets on freckles, vapor trails, sun flares, rainbows in vapor) to increase perceived realism and “alive” worldbuilding.

Use Seedance 2.5 inside CapCut to generate and edit your AI video in one place.

Leverage Seedance 2.5’s improved multilingual performance by prompting in your preferred language for better results.

### Use Seedance 2.5 in CapCut to generate and edit videos directly inside the app…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use Seedance 2.5 in CapCut to generate and edit videos directly inside the app without switching tools.  
Use timestamp-based storyline control to define what happens at specific times in your video.  
Use up to 50 reference inputs to better control the generated video’s look and content.  
Generate up to 90-second videos by creating a 30s base clip and then adding 2 extensions.  
Use viewport render workflows to preview/render within the viewport during creation.  
Use green screen workflows in CapCut with Seedance outputs for compositing and creator edits.  
Prompt or work in multiple languages to leverage Seedance 2.5’s improved multilingual performance.
```

Use up to 50 reference inputs to better control the generated video’s look and content.  
Generate up to 90-second videos by creating a 30s base clip and then adding 2 extensions.  
Use viewport render workflows to preview/render within the viewport during creation.  
Use green screen workflows in CapCut with Seedance outputs for compositing and creator edits.  
Prompt or work in multiple languages to leverage Seedance 2.5’s improved multilingual performance.

### Seedance 2.5で30秒作品を作るときは、推奨設定として16:9・30秒・最高品質・音声生成オンで一括生成する

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Seedance 2.5で30秒作品を作るときは、推奨設定として16:9・30秒・最高品質・音声生成オンで一括生成する  
Seedance 2.5では1回の生成に最大30枚の画像＋10本の動画＋10本の音声（合計最大50素材）を参照として入れ、プロンプト内で「この画像は主人公」「この動画は動き」「この音声は声」など役割を明示して制御する  
ホワイトモデル／クレイレンダーの簡易3D空間（人物位置・移動経路・カメラ角度・障害物・照明方向）を参照素材としてSeedance 2.5に渡し、実写/アニメとして再レンダリングして空間崩壊を減らす  
Seedance 2.5のタイムスタンプ編集を使い、0–5秒のカメラだけ変更・8–12秒のバッグだけ置換・15秒以降の服装だけ変更など「時間帯/要素を限定」して部分修正する  
30秒の中に出来事を詰め込みすぎず、重要イベントは1作品あたり5〜6個程度に抑えてモデルの注意分散を防ぐ  
日本語の長いセリフを30秒に詰めるより、短い一文（例：「間に合った」など）に絞って発音・口パク・演技の安定性を上げる
```

### Seedance 2.5は16:9・30秒・最高品質・音声生成オンで一括生成する前提でプロンプトと設定を組む

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Seedance 2.5は16:9・30秒・最高品質・音声生成オンで一括生成する前提でプロンプトと設定を組む  
Seedance 2.5では参照素材（画像/動画/音声/3Dなど）を入れるだけでなく、この画像は主人公・この動画は動き・この音声は声・この3Dモデルはカメラと人物配置のように役割をプロンプトで明示して渡す  
ホワイトモデル/クレイレンダーの簡易3D空間を先に作って人物位置・移動経路・カメラ角度・障害物・照明方向を固定し、それをSeedance 2.5に参照させて実写/アニメとして再レンダリングすると空間崩壊を減らせる  
Seedance 2.5のタイムスタンプ編集で0〜5秒のカメラだけ変更、8〜12秒のバッグだけ置換、15秒以降の服装だけ変更、最後のセリフだけ修正のように局所修正して全体再生成を避ける  
30秒の中に出来事を詰め込みすぎず、重要イベントは5〜6個程度に抑えるとSeedance 2.5の安定性が上がる  
日本語の長いセリフを30秒に詰め込まず、「間に合った」のような短い一文に絞ると発音・口パク・演技が安定しやすい  
30秒を延長して数分にする場合は、同じ登場人物・空間・映像トーンを維持する前提で延長生成を繰り返す設計で組む  
人物や小物の一貫性を上げるため、顔/髪型/服/持ち物（例：右手のケース）を全ショットで維持することをプロンプトで明文化し、左右の持ち替えやサイズ変化を禁止する  
物理破綻を減らすため、足の接地・水しぶき方向・ヒンジで開く・ラッチを左→右の順に外すなど因果関係のある動作手順をプロンプトに具体的に書く  
不要な生成物を避けるため、ロゴ/透かし/ランダム字幕/偶発文字/余計な人物/指の増殖/テレポート/過剰スローモーションをDo notで明示的に禁止する
```

### Seedance 2.5で30秒の完成映像を一発生成したい場合は、設定を16:9・30秒・最高品質・音声生成オンにする

![日本語](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-日本語-green)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Seedance 2.5で30秒の完成映像を一発生成したい場合は、設定を16:9・30秒・最高品質・音声生成オンにする  
Seedance 2.5では最大30枚の画像＋10本の動画＋10本の音声（合計最大50素材）を参照に入れ、プロンプトで「この画像は主人公」「この動画は動き」「この音声は声」など役割を明示して使い分ける  
Seedance 2.5で空間崩壊を減らすために、ホワイトモデル／クレイレンダー等の簡易3Dで人物位置・移動経路・カメラ角度・照明方向を先に固定し、その構造を参照させて実写／アニメとして再レンダリングする  
Seedance 2.5のタイムスタンプ編集を使い、「0〜5秒のカメラだけ変更」「8〜12秒のバッグだけ置換」「15秒以降の服装だけ変更」「最後のセリフだけ修正」のように時間帯や要素を指定して局所修正する  
Seedance 2.5で音声付き動画を安定させるため、日本語セリフは30秒に長文を詰めず「間に合った」など短い一文に絞る  
Seedance 2.5では30秒あるからと出来事を詰め込みすぎず、重要な出来事を5〜6個程度に抑えて注意分散による破綻を減らす
```

### Use @[char1] and @[char2] character tags to strictly lock each fighter’s face…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use @[char1] and @[char2] character tags to strictly lock each fighter’s face, hair, outfit, and silhouette across the whole Seedance 2.5 sequence.

Write your Seedance prompt as a numbered SEQUENCE with shot timestamps (e.g., Shot 1 / 0–2s … Shot 10 / 27–30s) to control pacing and continuity.

Specify camera behavior per shot (e.g., rear OTS WS, top-down FS, motivated whip pans, rolling dives) and explicitly state “a camera that never stops moving” to avoid static framing.

Define per-character color ownership (e.g., blue-white only for the male, crimson-orange only for the female) to prevent color swapping and keep readability.

Add an EXCLUDE block (e.g., no identity swap, duplication, color swap, static pose, slow motion, locked camera, realistic film look) to reduce common failure modes.

Include SOUND and VFX sections (e.g., pencil scratches, rough flicker, scribble trails, controlled smears, impacts tied to contact) to steer audio/FX style and keep effects physically motivated.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

Use @[char1] and @[char2] character tags to strictly lock each fighter’s face, hair, outfit, and silhouette across the whole Seedance 2.5 sequence.

Write your Seedance prompt as a numbered SEQUENCE with shot timestamps (e.g., Shot 1 / 0–2s … Shot 10 / 27–30s) to control pacing and continuity.

Specify camera behavior per shot (e.g., rear OTS WS, top-down FS, motivated whip pans, rolling dives) and explicitly state “a camera that never stops moving” to avoid static framing.

Define per-character color ownership (e.g., blue-white only for the male, crimson-orange only for the female) to prevent color swapping and keep readability.

Add an EXCLUDE block (e.g., no identity swap, duplication, color swap, static pose, slow motion, locked camera, realistic film look) to reduce common failure modes.

Include SOUND and VFX sections (e.g., pencil scratches, rough flicker, scribble trails, controlled smears, impacts tied to contact) to steer audio/FX style and keep effects physically motivated.

### Utilise 2 photos comme références d’identité (ex. un portrait studio + un…

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Utilise 2 photos comme références d’identité (ex. un portrait studio + un selfie) pour garder un visage cohérent sur toute la vidéo Seedance 2.5.  
Écris un prompt timestampé couvrant toute la durée (ex. 30 s) et détaille plan par plan les actions/cadrages (ex. rue sous la pluie, orbite 360°, rewind derrière la vitre, plongée dans la matrice, flatline) pour générer une séquence complète en une seule passe.  
Génère les 30 secondes d’un seul tenant avec Seedance 2.5 (sur Dreamina) pour maintenir la cohérence du personnage du premier au dernier plan.  
Fais le montage et le mix audio avec FFmpeg après génération (sans retouche image) pour assembler la vidéo et la musique.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

Utilise 2 photos comme références d’identité (ex. un portrait studio + un selfie) pour garder un visage cohérent sur toute la vidéo Seedance 2.5.  
Écris un prompt timestampé couvrant toute la durée (ex. 30 s) et détaille plan par plan les actions/cadrages (ex. rue sous la pluie, orbite 360°, rewind derrière la vitre, plongée dans la matrice, flatline) pour générer une séquence complète en une seule passe.  
Génère les 30 secondes d’un seul tenant avec Seedance 2.5 (sur Dreamina) pour maintenir la cohérence du personnage du premier au dernier plan.  
Fais le montage et le mix audio avec FFmpeg après génération (sans retouche image) pour assembler la vidéo et la musique.

### Use a Claude skill to break your script/topic into scenes and generate…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Use a Claude skill to break your script/topic into scenes and generate timestamped storyboard prompts matched to pacing before sending them to Seedance 2.5  
In Seedance 2.5, use 30-second continuous generation to produce longer, coherent shots per scene instead of many short clips  
Use Seedance 2.5 reference asset support to keep characters/locations/visual style consistent across scenes  
Output the final video in 16:9 format for a YouTube-ready vlog  
Add an AI voiceover that matches the script’s tone, then stitch scenes together and burn in captions automatically to produce an upload-ready vlog
```

### Test Seedance 2.5 with a 30-second, 720p generation to balance duration and…

![English](https://img.shields.io/badge/lang-English-blue)

#### 📝 Prompt

```
Test Seedance 2.5 with a 30-second, 720p generation to balance duration and output quality.  
Use 4 character sheets plus 1 location reference to keep characters and setting consistent across the video.  
Structure your prompt as Beat 1 → Beat 2 → Beat 3 to guide the narrative progression.  
For each Beat X, replace it with 2 precise keywords using the Arc wording technique to sharpen control over each segment.
```

#### 💡 Dicas

For each Beat X, replace it with 2 precise keywords using the Arc wording technique to sharpen control over each segment.
